Mathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Multiequalizer

CategoryTheory.Limits.HasMulticoequalizerdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Multiequalizer, predicate
Wording
I has multicoequalizers (from the name)
English
Let $I$ be a multispan index of $J$ and $C$. Then $I$ has multicoequalizers, if the multispan of $I$ has colimits.
Formula
$I \in \operatorname{MultispanIndex} (J , C)\vdash \operatorname{HasMulticoequalizer} (I):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{HasColimit} (\operatorname{multispan} (I))$
CategoryTheory.Limits.HasMultiequalizerdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Multiequalizer, predicate
Wording
I has multiequalizers (from the name)
English
Let $I$ be a multicospan index of $J$ and $C$. Then $I$ has multiequalizers, if the multicospan of $I$ has limits.
Formula
$I \in \operatorname{MulticospanIndex} (J , C)\vdash \operatorname{HasMultiequalizer} (I):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{HasLimit} (\operatorname{multicospan} (I))$
CategoryTheory.Limits.Multicoequalizer.descdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Multiequalizer, function or constant
Wording
the induced morphism of W and k (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $I$ be a multispan index of $J$ and $C$. Let $W$ be an element of $C$. Let $k$ be a function from elements $b$ of $\operatorname{R}$ applied to $J$ to homomorphisms of the right of $I$ and $b$ and $W$ such that for all elements $a$ of $\operatorname{L}$ applied to $J$, the composition of the first component of $I$ and $a$ and $k$ applied to the first map of $J$ and $a$ is equal to the composition of the second component of $I$ and $a$ and $k$ applied to the second map of $J$ and $a$. Then the induced morphism of $W$ and $b$ is a homomorphism of the multicoequalizer of $I$ and $W$ defined as the function that maps $h_{1}$ to the induced morphism of the multispan of $I$ and the projection of $W$ and $b$.
Formula
$I \in \operatorname{MultispanIndex} (J , C), W \in C , k \in \operatorname{R} (J)\rightarrow \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{right} (I , b), W), \forall a \in \operatorname{L} (J). \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{fst} (I , a), k (\operatorname{fst} (J , a)))}{\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{snd} (I , a), k (\operatorname{snd} (J , a)))}\vdash \operatorname{desc} (I , W , b , k , h_{1}):= \lambda h_{1} . \operatorname{desc} (\operatorname{multispan} (I), \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Limits\_\_Multicofork\_\_of\_u3c0\_} (I , W , b , k), h_{1})\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{multicoequalizer} (I), W)$

CategoryTheory.Limits.Multicoequalizer.multicoforkdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Multiequalizer, function or constant
Wording
the multicofork of I (from the name)
English
Let $I$ be a multispan index of $J$ and $C$. Then the multicofork of $I$ is a multicofork of $I$ defined as the cocone of the multispan of $I$.
Formula
$I \in \operatorname{MultispanIndex} (J , C)\vdash \operatorname{multicofork} (I):= \operatorname{cocone} (\operatorname{multispan} (I))\in \operatorname{Multicofork} (I)$

CategoryTheory.Limits.MulticoforkdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Multiequalizer, type
Wording
multicofork of I (from the name)
English
Let $I$ be a multispan index of $J$ and $C$. Then a multicofork of $I$ is an element of the function that maps $I$ to the set of cocones of the multispan of $I$.
Formula
$I \in \operatorname{MultispanIndex} (J , C)\vdash \operatorname{Multicofork} (I):= \lambda I . \operatorname{Cocone} (\operatorname{multispan} (I))$

Let $I$ be a multispan index of the product of $x_{1}$ and $C$. Then the linear order multispan isomorphism of $I$ is an isomorphism of the composition of the inclusion functor of $x_{1}$ and the multispan of $I$ and the multispan of the linear order of $I$.

CategoryTheory.Limits.cospanHomMkdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Pullback.Cospan, function or constant
Wording
the cospan homomorphism of l and r (from the name)
English

Mathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Pullback.Equalizer

CategoryTheory.Limits.equalizerPullbackMapIsodefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Limits.Shapes.Pullback.Equalizer, function or constant
Wording
the equalizer pullback map isomorphism of v (from the name)
English
Assume that the composition of $f$ and $t$ is equal to $s$. Assume that the composition of $g$ and $t$ is equal to $s$. Let $v$ be a homomorphism of $T$ and $S$. Then the equalizer pullback map isomorphism of $v$ is an isomorphism of the equalizer of the map of the identity morphism of $T$ and the identity morphism of $S$ and the map of the identity morphism of $T$ and the identity morphism of $S$ and the pullback of the inclusion of $f$ and $g$ and the first projection of the pullback of $s$ and $v$.
Formula
$\EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{comp} (f , t)}{s}, \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{comp} (g , t)}{s}, v \in \operatorname{Hom} (T , S)\vdash \operatorname{equalizerPullbackMapIso} (\mathrm{hf} , \mathrm{hg} , v)\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{equalizer} (\operatorname{map} (s , v , t , v , f , \operatorname{id} (T), \operatorname{id} (S), \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Limits\_\_equalizerPullbackMapIso\_\_\_proof\_1} (\mathrm{hf}), \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Limits\_\_equalizerPullbackMapIso\_\_\_proof\_2} (v)), \operatorname{map} (s , v , t , v , g , \operatorname{id} (T), \operatorname{id} (S), \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Limits\_\_equalizerPullbackMapIso\_\_\_proof\_1} (\mathrm{hg}), \mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Limits\_\_equalizerPullbackMapIso\_\_\_proof\_2} (v))), \operatorname{pullback} (\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Limits\_\_equalizer\_\_\_u3b9\_} (f , g), \operatorname{fst} (s , v)))$
